+          // Check emergency status on page load
+          async function checkEmergencyStatus() {
+            // 1. Apply cached value instantly (no flash)
+            try {
+              const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('u26_em_cache') || '{}');
+              if (cached.v === true) { emergencyMode = true; applyEmergencyMode(); }

+

+              const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('u26_em_cache') || '{}');

+                emergencyMode = !!cached.v;
+                applyEmergencyMode();

+            function initOverlayParticles(canvasId, overlayId) {
+              if (overlayId === 'reg-ov') {
+                const overlay = document.getElementById(overlayId);
+                if (overlay && !overlay.querySelector('.falling-items-container')) {
+                  initFallingRegItems(overlayId);

+              const overlay = document.getElementById(overlayId);
+              const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
+              if (!canvas || !overlay) return;
+              const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
+              const PARTICLE_COUNT = 30;
+              let particles = [];
+              let W, H;
+              function resize() {
+                if (!overlay.classList.contains('open')) return;
+                W = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
+                H = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
+              }
+              resize();
+              window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
+              function makeParticle() {
+                return {
+                  x: Math.random() * (W || window.innerWidth),
+                  y: Math.random() * (H || window.innerHeight),
+                  r: 1.2 + Math.random() * 2.5,
+                  vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3,
+                  vy: -(0.25 + Math.random() * 0.55),
+                  opacity: 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.55,
+                  opDir: Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1,
+                  blur: 1 + Math.random() * 3
+                };
+              }
+              for (let i = 0; i < PARTICLE_COUNT; i++) particles.push(makeParticle());
+              function draw() {
+                if (!overlay.classList.contains('open')) {
+                  requestAnimationFrame(draw);
+                  return;
+                }
+                if (!W || !H) resize();
+                ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
+                particles.forEach(p => {
+                  ctx.save();
+                  ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, p.opacity));
+                  ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(232, 84, 26, 0.9)';
+                  ctx.shadowBlur = p.blur * 8;
+                  ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, ${80 + Math.random() * 30 | 0}, 20, 1)`;
+                  ctx.beginPath();
+                  ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+                  ctx.fill();
+                  ctx.restore();
+                  p.x += p.vx;
+                  p.y += p.vy;
+                  p.opacity += 0.003 * p.opDir;
+                  if (p.opacity > 0.75 || p.opacity < 0.08) p.opDir *= -1;
+                  if (p.y < -8 || p.x < -8 || p.x > W + 8) {

+                  }
+                });
+                requestAnimationFrame(draw);
+              }
+              draw();

+            /* ======== DYNAMIC COMMITTEE + ACCESS FLAGS ======== */
+            async function fetchAccessFlags() {
+              try {
+                // Try cache first for instant UI
+                const cachedAccess = localStorage.getItem('u26_access_cache');
+                const cachedComm = localStorage.getItem('u26_comm_cache');
+                if (cachedAccess) {
+                  applyAccessFlags(JSON.parse(cachedAccess));
+                }
+                if (cachedComm) {
+                  const cached = JSON.parse(cachedComm);
+                  if (cached.length > 0) { COMMITTEE = cached; renderComm(); }
+                }
+
+                // Fetch fresh from sheet
+                if (!SHEETS_URL || SHEETS_URL.includes('YOUR_GOOGLE')) return;
+                const resp = await fetch(SHEETS_URL + '?action=getAccess');
+                const data = await resp.json();
+                if (!data.success) return;
+
+                // Apply access flags
+                if (data.access) {
+                  localStorage.setItem('u26_access_cache', JSON.stringify(data.access));
+                  applyAccessFlags(data.access);
+                }
+
+                // Apply committee data from sheet (overrides hardcoded)
+                if (data.committee && data.committee.length > 0) {
+                  COMMITTEE = data.committee.map(function (m) {
+                    return {
+                      isHead: String(m.ishead || m.IsHead || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE',
+                      name: String(m.name || m.Name || ''),
+                      role: String(m.role || m.Role || ''),
+                      dept: String(m.department || m.dept || m.Department || ''),
+                      img: String(m.photo || m.img || m.Photo || ''),
+                      imgStyle: String(m.imgstyle || m.ImgStyle || ''),
+                      phone: String(m.phone || m.Phone || ''),
+                      linkedin: String(m.linkedin || m.LinkedIn || '#'),
+                      hasGlow: String(m.hasglow || m.HasGlow || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'
+                    };
+                  });
+                  localStorage.setItem('u26_comm_cache', JSON.stringify(COMMITTEE));
+                  renderComm();
+                }
+                console.log('Access flags + Committee synced from sheet');
+              } catch (e) {
+                console.warn('fetchAccessFlags error (using fallback):', e);
+              }
+            }
+
+            function applyAccessFlags(flags) {
+              // Toggle Team button visibility
+              const teamBtn = document.getElementById('dock-committee');
+              if (teamBtn) {
+                const val = flags.show_team_btn;
+                // Hide if empty (""), false (boolean), "false" (string), "no", or missing
+                const isHidden = (!val || val === false || String(val).toLowerCase() === 'false' || String(val).toLowerCase() === 'no');
+
+                if (isHidden) {
+                  teamBtn.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
+                } else {
+                  teamBtn.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
+                }
+              }
+            }

+              const start = (e) => {
+                e.preventDefault();
+                portalTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+                  openPortal();
+                }, 1000);
+              };
+              const end = () => clearTimeout(portalTimer);
+              triggers.forEach(el => {
+                el.addEventListener('mousedown', start);

+                el.addEventListener('mouseup', end);
+                el.addEventListener('mouseleave', end);
+                el.addEventListener('touchend', end);
+              });

+            async function markTeamEntry() {

+                index: i, name: allMembersData[i]?.name || '', regno: allMembersData[i]?.regno || ''
+              }));

+                const payload = {
+                  action: 'handleTeamScan',
+                  uid: SCANNER_ID,

+                  data: {

+                    eventName: currentScanData.eventName,
+                    teamName: currentScanData.teamName || 'Solo',

+                  }
+                };
+                const resp = await fetch(SHEETS_URL, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(payload) });

+                    if (cb) cb.innerHTML = '<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>';

+                  });
